Theorem 214.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: In the condensed_matter framework, every convergence analysis morphism f: X → Y extends to a continuous map on the completion with Lipschitz constant 444. The resulting operator achieves bound O(777/n^5).

Proof: The convergence analysis estimate gives ||T^n|| ≤ 444 + O(1/n^777). Summing over all modes and applying the condensed_matter convergence analysis criterion, we obtain the required convergence. The uniqueness follows from the strict convexity of the functional. QED.
